Ravagli draws into a turn three [Great Sable Stag](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Great+Sable+Stag) and his opponent answers with a [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) . Four damage a turn is a lot though, especially as Marco is stuck on three lands. Four attacks and a [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) later we’re onto Game 3.

Luca Ravagli 2 - Marco Monetto 0

![](https://i.ormos.uk/30/7f/307f742aabc228987088519be18fe5b850258e2d88a7f4aadd6c6e5b404c9038.jpg)*The runner-up Marco Monetto mulliganed too much to stand a chance*Small sideboarding changes after game 1:  
 Monetto: + 2 [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout) ; - 1 [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) , - 1 [Doom Blade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Doom+Blade)  
 Ravagli: + 1 [Essence Scatter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Essence+Scatter) ; - 1 [Esper Charm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Esper+Charm)

The third game is equally unexciting, Monetto mulligans to four, starts with turn two [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) (again!). Ravagli only plays lands, and after the sixth token he [Volcanic Fallout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Fallout) s his opponent’s army and wins in two turns with two six point [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) s!

Luca Ravagli 3 - Marco Monetto 0

Congratulations to Luca Ravagli, 2009 Italian National Champion

## Finale - Marco Monetto (5C Faeries) - Luca Ravagli (Cruel Control)

*by Andrea Vitali*

Dopo due giorni gioco, dopo 16 match, siamo arrivati all’ultima fatica. Marco Monetto di Imperia, al suo primo nazionale potrebbe coronare il sogno di  
 diventare campione nazionale. Contro di lui Luca Ravagli di Pescara, che alla seconda presenza al nazionale vuole portare il titolo in Abruzzo.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e4/b8/e4b84253d6cbc87d7749a13b4125841b709d1e4e8ac90c0ff7f9c52763035bd1.jpg)*Marco Monetto, speriamo che ai mondiali sia più fortunato!*Entrambi i giocatori prendono un mulligan. buone le 6 per Marco che apre da faeries, mettendo [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) giù di seconda ed inizia ad attaccare coi token. Luca si limita ad evocare due [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) per due turni consecutivi, il primo con successo, il secondo neutralizzato da uno Spellslutter Sprite. Dopo qualche danno subito Luca si scoccia di subire e resetta il board con un [Volcanic Fallout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Fallout) . Stessa sorte il turno successivo per un [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) , ucciso dall’istant incounterabile. Quando Luca prova a porre a sua difesa un Plumveil, il muro viene terminato da un [Doom Blade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Doom+Blade) . Il suo [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) però rimane in gioco, nonostante il [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) di Marco, neutralizzato grazie ad un [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) . La comparsa dell’angelo eccita Luca, che inquietantemente inizia a ruggire, causando le risa del pubblico, e degli addetti ai lavori e dell’avversario, che si stropiccia gli occhiali. Nonostante il lifelink Luca deve faticare per tenersi in vita perchè il numero delle fatine che lo attaccano rimane costante, mentre i suoi life point, seppur di poco scendono costantemente. Sul 5 pari con Marco tutto tappato per una precedente Counter war di [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) , Luca risolve un [Bogardan Hellkite](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bogardan+Hellkite) , che gli consegna la prima partita.

Monetto 0: + 4 [Anathemancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Anathemancer) , + 1 [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) ; - 3 [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout) , - 1 [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) , - 1 [Sower of Temptation](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sower+of+Temptation)  
 Ravagli 1: +4 [Great Sable Stag](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Great+Sable+Stag) , +2 [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) +1 Path of Exile; -3 [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) , -3 [Hallowed Burial](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hallowed+Burial) , -1 [Cruel Ultimatum](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cruel+Ultimatum)

Sfortunato Marco in partenza della seconda partita quando deve mulliganare ben 2 volte. Luca risolve un [Great Sable Stag](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Great+Sable+Stag) , e quando Marco cala [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) esclama: “Così muori prima!”. In un game che sembra non avere storia, le note divertenti sono i versi che accompagnano l’alce durante le fasi di attacco: a volte ruggisce, a volte miagola, a volte emette suoni robotici o di astronavi in decollo. Di certo c’è che per Luca stanno preparando, dopo la cerimonia di premiazione, una bella camicia bianca, che si allaccia davanti stretta stretta e con le maniche di dietro! Quando Marco si tappa per lanciare un [Anathemancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Anathemancer) , Luca mostra un [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) chiedendo: “ facciamo la prossima?”. Ovviamente l’avversario non può fare altro che ammucchiare le carte per passare alla prossima.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/30/7f/307f742aabc228987088519be18fe5b850258e2d88a7f4aadd6c6e5b404c9038.jpg)*Luca Ravagli che ringrazia sentitamente Ciro!*Monetto 0: + 2 [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout) ; - 1 [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) , - 1 [Doom Blade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Doom+Blade)  
 Ravagli 2: + 1 [Essence Scatter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Essence+Scatter) ; - 1 [Esper Charm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Esper+Charm)

Dopo un triplo mulligan (4 carte) Marco riesce a fare l’apertura da faeries mettendo [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) di seconda. Luca non fa assolutamente nulla e continua a prendere danni dei token prodotto dall’incantesimo fino a quando non trova un [Volcanic Fallout](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Fallout) , fermando l’emorragia delle vite a 8.  
 Luca prova a vincere la partita con un [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) da 7, mandando Marco a 5 (danni subiti esclusivamente a causa di [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) ). Monetto cerca disperatamente una soluzione per vincere la race di danni. Spara un fulmine a fine turno, e mette in gioco Spellslutter Sprite. Purtroppo per lui sulla libreria non si presenta nessuna soluzione per vincere nel turno e quando passa a Luca basta lanciare un secondo [Banefire](https://web.archive.org/web/20220928214238/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Banefire) , diventando il campione italiano 2009. Compliementi comunque ad entrambi per lo spettacolo offerto!

Marco Monetto 0 - Luca Ravagli 3
